# r271 section 29 evidence (2026-11-09): twin growth-rate comparison and the limited momentum carrier. Tools: tools/fire/r271/gr_*.py, kh_state.py, xp_ownmean.py, xp_gridshare.py.
# gr_analyze outputs keep the growth-rate fits; arm ratios (same for every fit window of a run set) are kept once per set. Per-frame band tables are regenerable from the run dirs and are dropped.

######## sec29_notes.md
# s29 notes (2026-11-09): growth-rate (twin) comparison

Method: same base state (FDS 9.75 s), each code control + perturbed (gr_perturb.py: discrete MAC curl of smoothed random A,
div ~1e-16; 2 mm/s rms r<0.15 k0-16; x4 arm for linearity). FDS dt locked 1.646 ms (else frame times drift). RISE checkpoints
step-locked (build_bt.py RISE_R271_CKPT_EVERY; wall-clock retention gives different steps per arm). Analysis gr_analyze.py
(bands, azimuthal m0-4, scale L>=8dx / M / G<2.7dx, delta-T), gr_tbins.py (by control T), gr_div.py (exact MAC divergence).

Checks: FDS duplicate control bitwise identical. Linear: FDS e4/e1 within 10% to 0.4 s; RISE within 2-6% to 0.6 s.

1. 0.6 s, production SGS (gr_fit_*.txt): R/F whole-column dE ratio 1.0 at 0.05 s, 0.96 at 0.10, 0.34 at 0.29, 0.065 at 0.59.
   Fits 0.02-0.15 / 0.15-0.35 / 0.35-0.6: RISE 8.5/4.7/4.9, FDS 9.4/7.6/7.6 (1/s, 0.5 dlnE/dt).
2. Base strain (base_strain.txt): RISE mean strain falls only 3-10% -> not a weaker base.
3. Fine cadence 0.2 s (gr3_*, gr5_*): fit 0.025-0.1 prod: E 8.7 vs 9.1, G 19.1 vs 23.7; SGS off E 10.1 vs 12.2, G 20.7 vs 28.2.
   Ratio R/F at 0.1 / 0.2 s: prod 0.96/0.53, SGS off 0.74/0.10 -> resolved numerics, not SGS.
4. By control T (gr_tbins.txt, FDS T +273.15 fixed): at 0.1 s prod 300-350 K 0.86, 350-600 0.73, 600-900 0.79, >900 1.5
   (SGS off 0.97/0.53/0.61/1.2). Deficit in the mixing layer, not the core.
5. Divergence (gr_div.txt): base rms D and dD per T bin equal within ~10% -> not the EOS/divergence path.
6. Isolated sub-3-cell (cold): advmom n=2/2.5 no decay, c/U 0.228 vs 0.229; TG m55/m60 (2.5/2.3 cells) A 0.940 vs 0.941.
7. FDS twin ablations SGS off (gr5): whole column 0.1-0.2 s: FDS 19.1, CENTRAL 42, GODUNOV 17.4, no baroclinic 20.3, RISE 8.4.
   Limiter changes FDS T growth (CENTRAL 28 / CHARM 19 / GODUNOV 13.5 vs RISE 15 early) but not enough for velocity.
8. Cold uniform-density closed tanh shear layer (kh_state.py, SGS off): RISE = FDS to 1-4% in E, all m, L, M -> deficit needs density variation.
9. Momentum carrier: production compatible stage rate uses the CENTRED carrier rho_face*v + physical (fct_centered_momentum_carrier),
   while density moves with the FCT-limited flux low+alpha*delta; velocity = momentum / face density. Inconsistent at fronts.
   Opt-in RISE_FIRE_LIMITED_MOMENTUM_CARRIER=1 (AdvectionMac host dispatch). Default path bitwise identical (grlc_ref vs H_nosgs).
   SGS off twin 0.1-0.2 s whole column: default 8.4, limited 14.0, FDS 19.1; 4-9: 2.6/5.7/6.8; 10-19: 6.1/11.7/13.4.
   Base grid share k30: default 0.044, limited 0.124/0.095, FDS 0.096/0.090; max|w| bounded.
   Pending: production transplant xp_lc (2.5 s) and production twin grlcp_*.
